      "area": "The pump as a world object on the island: the template a Hive node has to mirror (My Dirty Little Lake/scripts/pump.gd, and every place in lake.gd, player.gd, dog.gd, flora.gd, wildlife.gd, puddles.gd, weather.gd and tools/ that asks about the pump)",
      "summary": "The pump (`class_name Pump extends Node2D`, scripts/pump.gd, 131 lines) is a front-on picture on the island's lawn. Its position is a static, `Pump.tile`, and walkers query it through static `covers()`/`hides()` instead of being handed a reference. `Lake._ready` builds it on CRATE_LAYER 8 and sets the static before `_grow_nature()` and `_start_weather()`, whose `_ready`s sow flora, find shore spots and lay puddles using that static. It is wired into 12 functions in 6 scripts:\n- lake.gd: `_walker_layer`, `_weather_roofs`, `_weather_statics`, `_draw_shed_lamp`, the desk and pad interact chains, `_at_pump`\n- player.gd: `_slide`, `_can_stand`\n- dog.gd: `_bumped`, `_may_stand`\n- flora.gd: `_sow`\n- wildlife.gd: `_add_shore`, `_on_sand`\n- puddles.gd: `may_lie`\n\nTests: `test_lake._stage_wash` (lines 7902-7928) and the flora check at 6446. Probe: `tools/shot_pump.gd`.\n\nA Hive at about tile (43.3, 47.3), which is `Iso.shed_centre()` (45.320, 45.320) + (-2.02, +1.98), mirrors all of these. That spot is 128 world px left of the hut and level with its middle. It is 2.36 tiles from the door stand (45.55, 46.60) and 4.8 tiles from the pump (47.42, 44.82), so its E range cannot overlap either.\n\nThe pump has one scalar square footprint, centres its picture horizontally on its foot, bakes one image, and keys its shadow cache on the box alone. None of that holds for a hive+shelf sprite with several states. Some of the pump's wiring also has latent bugs that should not be copied: the wash room leaves the angler free to walk, U/pad-Y opens the shop over it, `roof()` copies an Image every frame, and the hut slide measures from the wrong centre. There are also three stale comment/doc contradictions to name before building on them.",

        "**The static must be set before `_grow_nature()` / `_start_weather()`** (lake.gd:1216-1217).         "**A new `class_name Hive` must reach Godot's global class cache** (a run with `<exe> --path . --headless --import`, or an editor rescan). Until then every script that says `Hive.covers` (player.gd, dog.gd, flora.gd, wildlife.gd, puddles.gd, lake.gd) fails to parse in a headless test_lake run, and parse errors only show in `--log-file`.         "**The pump's footprint is one scalar square (`FOOT_HALF` on both axes)**. A hive with a shelf running up-left along -tx is a rectangle, or hive and shelf are two boxes. With the pump's square:\n- walkers walk through the shelf posts;\n- `_walker_layer`'s 'north of both near faces' rule misdraws a walker standing just in front of the shelf, putting it behind the whole sprite.\n\nUse Vector2 half-extents centred on the combined base, consistently in `covers`, `_walker_layer`, `_bumped`, `_slide` and the tests.",

        "**Shade.sweep and Skirt.hem treat anything whose lowest pixel is well above the picture's deepest row as an overhang.** Sweep uses its `ground` band; hem uses `HEM_BAND` = 0.3 of the height.       "area": "My Dirty Little Lake/scripts/wash_stand.gd (plus the parts of wash_room.gd, pad.gd, lake.gd, test_lake.gd and the probes that drive it), read to see what an 'uncap' minigame in the hive room can reuse",
      "summary": "WashStand (class_name WashStand, extends Control, 1402 lines) is one self-contained Control that does five things:\n\n1. **Reveal mask.** Two parallel arrays over the restored find, `_solid` (PackedByteArray) and `_grime` (PackedFloat32Array). The grid is FINE (2) cells per painted pixel each way. A cols x rows RGBA8 Image, `_coat`, holds one pixel per cell and is drawn scaled, nearest-filtered, over the art. It is repainted one pixel at a time, and only when a cell crosses a step line. It is pushed to the GPU once a frame (`_coat_tex.update`).\n2. **Jet.** Radial brush `_wash_at`, with trickles, spray flecks and bubbles simulated by hand.\n3. **Finish machine.** WASHING -> RINSING -> SHINING -> CLEAN. The `washed` signal fires when SHINING starts.\n4. **Nozzle and hose.** Eased drawing only. The jet itself lands on the raw `_aim`.\n5. **Hose loop.** An OGG loop with a code-built band-noise fallback.\n\nInput: the mouse goes through `_gui_input`, gated by a wake latch (`awake()`, WAKE_AFTER 0.4 s after the choosing press is let go). The pad goes through `WashRoom._pad_aim`, which moves the real hidden pointer with `Pad.move_cursor(left stick)` and calls `stand.spray(get_local_mouse_position(), RT or A held)`. `spray()` itself is never gated, so harnesses and probes drive it directly.\n\nThe cleanest thing to lift for an uncap minigame (a hot knife revealing honey under wax caps) is the mask alone. That means a RefCounted with no node, no input, no drawing and no sound. It holds `solid`, `amount`, `total`, `left`, the coat Image/ImageTexture, the step cuts, and `wear`, `brush`, `share_clean` and `flush`. It takes the host's RNG and an ink Callable, so the wash's randomness and pixels stay exactly the same.\n\nTo leave wash behaviour and the existing tests and probes untouched, WashStand must keep its private names (`_solid`, `_art`, `_region`, `_zoom`, `_grow`, `_fit`, `_stand_top`, `_stand_pad`, `_await_release`, `_asleep`, `_drive_wake`), because tests and probes reach them with `get`/`set`/`call`.\n\nEverything else stays in WashStand: nozzle, hose, stream, runs, flecks, stand drawing. The wake gate and the loop-voice loader are small enough to copy rather than share.",

      ],
      "pitfalls": [
        "Packed arrays are value types in GDScript 4. If `_solid`/`_grime` become getters returning a helper's arrays and WashStand code still writes `_solid[i] = ...` or `_grime[i] = ...`, the write goes to a copy and is lost silently. Every write (`_read_solid`, `_roll_coat`, `_wear`, the rinse) must live inside the helper. Per-cell reads through a getter in hot loops (`_wash_at`, `_drive_finish`, `_step_run`) also add a call per cell, so move those loops too.",
        "Tests and probes reach private names by reflection: test_lake sets `_await_release`/`_asleep` and calls `_drive_wake`/`_fit`/`_stand_top`/`_stand_pad`/`_process`, reads `_zoom`/`_region` and sets `_grow`. shot_pump.gd reads `_art`/`_solid`/`_region`. `Object.set` on a missing name is a silent no-op, so a check then fails with a misleading message. `get` returns null, and shot_pump's `var solid: PackedByteArray = ...get(&'_solid')` would throw in a desktop probe, which looks like a hang.         "One RNG drives everything: `_roll` is seeded with `hash(String(piece))`, gives the coat's noise seed, and then feeds the bubble rolls inside `_wear`, the run/fleck spawns and the stars, all interleaved.         "Float precision: `_total` sums float64 amounts, `_grime` stores float32, and `_left` subtracts float32-derived values.       "pitfalls": [
        "The wildlife moment's zoom-in is overwritten every frame: `_moment_step` writes `_camera.zoom`, and later in the same `_process` `if _glide < 0.0: _push_zoom()` (lake.gd:5266-5267) resets it to `_view_zoom`. In play the moment pans but does not zoom. `_check_signals` misses it because it calls `_moment_step` directly. A hive moment built on this inherits the bug; guard with `_moment < 0.0`.",
        "The moment is single-slot (`_moment_owed`). A hive arrival written into the same slot while a wildlife moment is owed (the player is in a board) silently drops the wildlife one. `_wildlife_seen` stays false and `first_arrived` only fires again on a none->some turn, which is effectively never mid-run.",
        "`_start_owed_moment` hard-codes `_wildlife_seen = true` and `Text.WILDLIFE_BACK` and calls `save_game()`. Reusing it as-is marks wildlife seen on a hive arrival.",
        "`Vector2.ZERO` is a legitimate `_first_spot()` fallback; `Vector2.INF` is the owed sentinel. Do not use ZERO for 'none'.",
        "The hive's absent-key rule is the opposite of every existing flag, which reads absent as done. So the var default must be the empty state, and `load_game` must assign it unconditionally from `save.get(...)`. Otherwise a load onto a live lake (harness, backup fallback) keeps stale hive state. Watch finished saves too: Richard's farewell=true save will load with the arc 'not started', and with a clean island the swarm can come almost at once.",
        "`load_game` returns false before touching any state on a version or seed mismatch or a failed `grid.restore`. Do not put hive reads ahead of those returns.",
        "Flora is not saved and is rebuilt from the fresh map in `_grow_nature` (1218, before `load_game` at 1365). The real stage only arrives on the first post-load remap (`_filth_stale` 6367, FILTH_REMAP 0.2 s, threaded), and plants then take up to about 6 s to read as grown. Any check that derives or regresses hive state from flora right after a load will see about 0 hosts.",
        "`Flora.refresh` only re-evaluates due plants when a piece leaves the water (a remap). Ages advance in Flora._process, which switches itself off when nothing grows and there are no bees. Poll the island count on a timer in the lake; do not hook refresh.",
        "`alive_count()` counts due, not grown, plants of every kind, lawn to open water. It is not an island bee-host count.",
        "Island flora depends on the thin-ring water beside each plant being clean (ray out from ISLAND_CENTRE) AND on the GLOBAL clean share (rank < stage*0.55). The count can sit low for a long time on a lake cleaned unevenly, and in the harness it is driven by whatever `_stage_nature` emptied (the west half).",

        "`Flora.avoid` only keeps open-water beds off the yards. It is not a general exclusion list, so adding the hive there does nothing for island plants.",

        {
          "topic": "How to build a short procedural sound in the house style (recovered from git history)",
          "detail": "The deleted builders are the template. `git show bb74f8b^:\"My Dirty Little Lake/scripts/sfx.gd\"` lines 312-819 hold:\n- `_make_splash`: noise through one-pole `lerpf` filters plus a falling-pitch knock.\n- `_make_pop`: a 75 ms burst `randf*exp(-t*190)` through two resonators, `_resonator(1650,1100)` and `(520,420)`, plus a 190 Hz thump, with a 1.5 ms attack and a `pow(1-t/len,1.8)` release.\n- `_make_engine`: a 0.5 s LOOP whose frequencies are all multiples of 2 Hz so the seam is whole cycles.\n- `_make_drag`: a loop of cascaded one-poles with slow swells.\n- `_make_wings`: humps of resonated noise.\n- `_make_coo`: a phase accumulator with a bend and flutter.\n- `_make_chink` and `_make_bought`: inharmonic partials plus a noise tick.\n\n`678dd1e^` lines 989-1085 hold `_fire`, `_resonator`, `_ring`, `_make_chime` and `_to_wav`.\n\nThe shared helpers, to re-add:\n```\nfunc _resonator(hz, bw) -> Array: var d := exp(-PI*bw/RATE); return [2.0*d*cos(TAU*hz/RATE), -d*d, 0.0, 0.0]\nfunc _ring(f: Array, s: float) -> float: var o: float = s + f[0]*f[2] + f[1]*f[3]; f[3] = f[2]; f[2] = o; return o\n```\nEach builder follows the same pattern: `count := int(len*RATE)`, fill a PackedFloat32Array, track `peak`, rescale to 0.7-0.85, then `return _to_wav(out, looping)`.\n\nSuggested hive builders:\n- **Buzz or hum (loop):** a sawtooth-ish sum of 4-6 partials at a root of about 225 Hz, times a slow amplitude wobble, plus `_ring(_resonator(240,180), noise)*0.03`. Every frequency must be a multiple of 1/length (0.5 Hz for a 2 s loop), and the noise crossfaded tail-over-head the way `_noise_hiss` does it.\n- **Sizzle (uncapping, loop):** high-passed noise `(n - low)` with `low = lerpf(low, n, 0.5)`, plus random crackle impulses rung through a 3 kHz resonator, crossfaded.\n- **Whirr (extractor, loop):** noise through 2-3 cascaded lerps at about 0.05, times a rotation amplitude modulation at an integer number of cycles per loop, plus a 90 Hz sine and a resonator click once per turn. Its runtime `pitch_scale` follows crank speed, as `_drive_hiss` does.\n- **Glug (pour, one-shot of about 0.6 s):** 3-5 bubbles, each a noise burst through a resonator swept up from about 300 to 700 Hz over 40 ms, at irregular times. Build 2-3 seeds as variants and pick with `next_step`.\n- **Pop (jar lid):** reuse `_make_pop`, optionally with one short 2 kHz partial for a lid tink.\n- **Smoke puff:** a noise hump through `_resonator(300,700)`, as in `_make_wings`.\n\nSeed a local RandomNumberGenerator. `Sfx._rng` is randomised, so builds from it differ every session.\n\nMeasured cost (Godot 4.7.1 headless, this machine): additive synthesis is about 23 ms per second of audio at 8 partials; filtered noise is about 4 ms per second; the pop is 0.6 ms; `_to_wav` is about 1 ms per second.",
          "refs": "git show bb74f8b^:\"My Dirty Little Lake/scripts/sfx.gd\" (312-819); git show 678dd1e^:\"My Dirty Little Lake/scripts/sfx.gd\" (989-1085); scripts/wash_stand.gd:1330-1359; scratch bench"

      "pitfalls": [
        "A name added to `Sfx.SOUNDS` with no file and no built stream in `_streams` fails test_lake's check that every recording the sound board names is loaded (test_lake.gd:2538). Built fallbacks must be put in `_streams` during `_ready`, not lazily on first play.",
        "`_load_recordings` sets a BEDS WAV's `loop_end = data.size()/4`, which assumes stereo 16-bit. A mono built loop passed through that branch loops at half length. Insert built streams after the file pass, or set the loop yourself; `_to_wav(samples, true)` already sets it correctly.",
        "Never send a looping stream through `play()`. It lands on a pooled or CHANNELS voice that never goes idle, and the pool loses a voice for good. Loops need their own player: a `_bed()` player in Sfx, or a room-owned player like WashStand's `_hiss`.",
        "A new `AudioStreamPlayer` defaults to the \"Master\" bus and bypasses the SFX and Ambience sliders. Set `.bus` explicitly (`Prefs.BUS_SFX` or `Prefs.BUS_AMBIENCE`). test_lake fails any Sfx child player on another bus (test_lake.gd:2666-2672).",
        "`_bed()` never asks `may_play`. A hive hum bed must gate on `not shopping and not indoors` itself, or it plays inside the shed and behind the upgrades board. It must also be cleared in `hush()`, or it carries over past the lake's exit.",
        "`play_bee()` spends the `&\"bee\"` `_due` gap that Flora's bees share (sfx.gd:922, flora.gd:283-288). Hive sounds need their own keys. `_gap` and `_due` run on the wall clock (`Time.get_ticks_msec`), so a test that plays twice must erase `_next_due` or `_last` first.",
        "`Sfx._rng` is randomised in `_ready`, so built takes drawn from it differ every session. Use a seeded local RandomNumberGenerator, as `_noise_hiss` does with `seed = 37`.",
        "Loop seams: every sinusoid in a built loop must fit whole cycles (frequency × length an integer), and filtered noise must be crossfaded tail-over-head (`_noise_hiss`). The old engine's fade-to-zero at the seam dips.",
        "Built sounds are peak-normalised (0.7-0.85), not levelled to -22 LUFS like the recordings. Their SOUNDS dB are by-ear numbers and must be re-tuned when a real recording drops in; build_sfx's report shift column does not cover them.",
        "Boot cost: Sfx is an autoload, and its `_ready` runs as the engine starts. Additive synthesis costs about 23 ms per second of audio at 8 partials; filtered noise costs about 4 ms per second. Keep built takes short and use few partials.",
        "The hive room is outdoors like the wash room. `Sfx.indoors` must stay tied to `_shed_open`, and the room's own sounds must not be added to `WHILE_INDOORS` for that reason.       "pitfalls": [
        "A board not in `Pad.FOCUS_GROUP` with a non-empty `pad_focus()` (or `pad_free()` true) is unusable on a gamepad. Pad mode hides the mouse, and nothing moves the pointer unless the board calls `Pad.move_cursor`; the right-stick fallback the pad.gd header mentions is gone. A still sends a left click wherever the hidden pointer was left.",
        "Forgetting `or _hive_open` in `Lake.pad_cursor_wanted()` breaks the pad in the room in two ways. Pad drops focus, so A/B/LB/RB are not converted. And `_pad_buttons` runs A as interact (pump, shed, dog) under the open room.",
        "Forgetting the hive in the Escape chain (lake.gd:2096-2113) means Escape, and pad B which is a synthetic Escape, falls through to `_set_settings(true)`, opening the settings over the hive instead of closing it.",
        "`_start_owed_moment` hard-codes `Text.WILDLIFE_BACK` and sets `_wildlife_seen = true` (lake.gd:5152, 5160). Routing the swarm through it unchanged shows the wildlife words and silently consumes the wildlife moment. `_moment_owed` is a single Vector2 slot, so a second owed moment overwrites the first. The moment also waits while `_panelled()`, so it will not start while the hive room is open.",
        "Use `set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(PRESET_FULL_RECT)` (WashRoom) or size by hand from `get_viewport_rect().size` on `size_changed` (MomentCard, FocusRing, TourCard). Plain `set_anchors_preset` keeps a new Control at zero size under a CanvasLayer; TourCard's first capture had no card for exactly this reason.",
        "Style.write puts a black shade under any ink with luminance over SHADE_UNDER (0.3). `PAPER_SOFT` (0.369,0.302,0.235) computes to about 0.311, so `Style.write(..., Style.PAPER_SOFT)` smudges on paper. wash_room.gd:340-345 and shed_shelf.gd:59-65 already do this. `PAPER_INK` and `PAPER_HEAD` are under 0.3 and safe. For soft ink use `draw_string` directly, as TourCard does.",
        "`Style.write` neither wraps nor clips, and its `at.y` is the baseline, not the top. Wrap with `FirstSteps._wrap(text, Style.font(), size_px, wide)`, and cut or shrink by measuring with `Style.measure`. The Letter counts overruns and test_lake asks for zero.",
        "CloseButton emits `pressed` on the mouse PRESS and makes no click sound; PlankButton plays `ui_click` on press and emits `pressed` on RELEASE, and only if still held. Do not double up sounds: `Lake._shut` already plays `ui_close`.",
        "Sibling order is click priority. A full-rect STOP child (the stage, like WashStand) added after the tray, planks or cross eats their clicks. Add it before them, add the CloseButton last, or override `_has_point` to limit a board's hit area.",
        "Rooms and cards share the HUD CanvasLayer (layer 1), where the latest node in the tree wins both drawing and focus. A lazily built hive room added after a TourCard, MomentCard or haul count covers it; the lake keeps the tour card last with `move_child`.",
        "`board_ribbon` expects its box centred on the board's top edge: the plank's foot is set `BORDER_TOP` under the box's middle. Also compute `close_on` and `title_room` from the same ribbon box, or the cross and the title drift apart. Painted wood only fits boxes of at least `BORDER_LEAST` (32x32); smaller PlankButtons and planks fall back to the drawn `Style.plank`, a visibly different wood.",
        "`pad_focus` boxes must be in the focus node's own local pixels. A child's `get_rect()` only works for direct children of that node; nested controls need converting. Keys are compared with `==` to re-find the pick after a re-layout, so keep them stable (StringName), not indices, when the list changes between steps.",
        "A `pad_press(key)` that returns true for keys it does not handle (ShedRoom's returns true whenever the shelf is shut) stops A from ever becoming a click on those controls. In free mode `pad_press` is called with a null key.",
        "The only Kenney prompt tiles are the 13 listed; there is no E, B, X, Y or shoulder tile. `FirstSteps._tex` or `TourCard._load` return null for a missing name and the icon is silently not drawn. Add tiles through `tools/build_prompts.py` PICKS and reimport.",
        "The project sets no default canvas texture filter, so Godot's default is Linear. TourCard, FirstSteps and MomentCard do not set NEAREST, while PlankButton, ShedRoom and the wash Tray do. Set `texture_filter = NEAREST` on any new Control that draws prompt tiles or pixel art, or it may soften at a 1.5 stretch.",
        "A new CSV key reads back as its own key name until `tools/build_translations.py` has run AND the project has been reimported: the `.translation` files are built on import and git-ignored. Never edit scripts/text.gd by hand, since the next build overwrites it. The harness must set English through TranslationServer and `Style.set_locale`, never `Prefs.set_language`, which writes settings.cfg.",
        "A click that falls through the room to `Lake._unhandled_input` while `_panelled()` closes the controls, settings, menu and shed boards (lake.gd:2200-2204). Keep the room full-rect with MOUSE_FILTER_STOP, as WashRoom does, so no click reaches the lake.",
        "WashStand-style tools wait for the choosing press to be released and then `WAKE_AFTER` (0.4 s) before taking input (`awake()`). A hive step that starts on the same click or A press that chose it will fire at once unless it copies that wait."

      "area": "Art pipeline and island drawing (build_pump/build_nozzle/build_wash_backdrop conventions, Skirt, Shade, Iso helpers, island z layers in lake.gd, pump anchoring/scale, hive_mockup.py split into a build_hive.py builder)",
      "summary": "The pump is the model to copy. `tools/build_pump.py` authors the picture as letter rows, one per painted pixel, inks it, rings it with a 1 px outline and writes `assets/pump.png` plus `pump.json` {size, ground}. `scripts/pump.gd` draws it at ART_SCALE 2.0 with the foot at the node origin: box = Rect2(-w/2, -h*(1-ground), w, h). It swept-shades the silhouette with a lazily made `Shade.Cast` child, bakes one `Skirt.hem` grass patch, and exposes statics (`tile`, `covers`, `hides`) that the walkers, flora, puddles and wildlife ask.\n\nIn `lake.gd` the pump sits on CRATE_LAYER (8). `_walker_layer` puts a walker north of it on BEHIND_CRATE (7). `_at_pump()` is asked before `_at_shed()` in both interact paths, and `_draw_shed_lamp` moves the lamp onto the pump.\n\nAt tile (43.3, 47.3) the hive is on lawn (2.14 tiles in), with no screen overlap with the hut: its foot is 128 px left of the hut's middle and the hut's half-width is 73.5 px.
